CONNETQUOT RIVER NEAR NORTH GREAT RIVER NY, USGS site 01306499 (USGS-01306499), stream in Suffolk County, New York. Daily means from 1979-08-22 to 2015-09-29, a page a water year (October to September), linked below. Discharge in cubic feet a second, gage height in feet above the gauge's datum. Its annual peaks are its floods; its field measurements are the visits that check the gauge. Revisions are USGS's own notes of the site's published history, as written.
